    OVERVIEW                                                             概覽
    We are the leading and a rapidly growing home meal products          我們是中國領先且快速增長的在家吃飯餐食
    brand in China. We offer a variety of ready-to-eat, ready-to-heat,   產品品牌。我們提供即食、即熱、即煮和即
    ready-to-cook and prepared ingredients, with a focus on at-home      配食材，並專注於在家火鍋和燒烤產品。憑
    hotpot and barbecue products. With a carefully curated product       藉精心策劃的產品組合和廣泛的社區門店網
    portfolio and an extensive network of community-based stores,        絡，我們為消費者提供產品，使他們能夠在
    we enable consumers to enjoy meals at home with tastiness,           家中享用好吃、方便還不貴的餐食產品。憑
    convenience and affordability. Leveraging our robust supply          藉我們強大的供應鏈能力以及遍佈全國的零
    chain and production capabilities and a nationwide network of        售門店，我們使用鍋圈食匯品牌提供各種餐
    retail stores, we offer a wide variety of home meal products under   食產品，服務於不同的用餐場景。
    the Guoquan Shihui (鍋圈食匯) brand, serving diverse dining
    scenarios.


    We are devoted to developing products under our proprietary          我們致力於開發帶有「鍋圈食匯」標誌的自有
    brand name carrying the “Guoquan Shihui” logo. As the leading        品牌產品。作為中國領先的一站式在家吃飯
    one-stop home meal products brand in China, we are well              餐食產品品牌，我們具備把握中國在家吃飯
    positioned to capture the significant growth potential in China’s    餐食產品市場巨大增長潛力的能力，並以優
    home meal products market. We cater to different dining              質食材為基礎，具有創造新產品及多樣化產
    scenarios underpinned by high quality food ingredients that have     品的巨大潛力，滿足不同的用餐場景。我們
    an immense potential for creating new and diverse products. Our      的產品組合包括八大類別，涵蓋火鍋產品、
    product portfolio comprises eight categories including hotpot        燒烤產品、飲品、一人食、即烹餐包、生
    products, barbecue products, beverages, solo-dining meals,           鮮、西餐及零食。
    ready-to-cook meal kits, fresh produce, western cuisines and
    snacks.


    We have established a store network primarily comprising             我們已建立主要包括加盟店的門店網絡。在
    franchised stores. Under our franchise business model, we derive     我們的特許經營業務模式下，我們的絕大部
    substantially all of our revenue from the sale of our products to    分收益來自向加盟商銷售我們的產品，加盟
    franchisees, who open and operate franchised stores under our        商以我們的品牌開設及經營加盟店並向消費
    brand and sell our products to consumers. We do not charge or        者銷售我們的產品。我們並不收取亦不依賴
    rely on franchise fees.                                              於加盟費。


    We have also been developing online sales channels including our     我們亦開發線上銷售渠道，包括我們的鍋圈
    Guoquan APP, WeChat mini-program as well as on popular social        APP、微信小程序以及流行社交商務平台（如
    commerce platforms such as Douyin. In addition, we partner with      抖音）。此外，我們與美团及餓了麼等第三
    third-party food delivery platforms, such as Meituan and Ele.me      方外賣平台合作，為消費者提供外賣到家服
    to deliver our products to consumers. We achieve highly extensive    務。線上線下的購物選擇，實現了廣泛的消
    consumer reach by providing both online and offline shopping         費者觸達。憑藉供應鏈及數字化管理體系的
    options. Leveraging the highly efficient management and              高效管理及運營，我們能夠確保產品質量及
    operation of our supply chain and our digitalized management         安全並實現從食品生產到零售的高運營效率。
    system, we are able to ensure product quality and safety, as well
    as achieve high operational efficiency from food production to
    retail sales.

公司介紹


OUR VISION                                                         我們的願景
Our vision is to become the go-to brand for dining at home in      我們的願景是在家吃就鍋圈。
China.


OUR MISSION                                                        我們的使命
Our mission is to source quality food ingredients globally and     我們的使命是匯聚全球好食材，多場景提供
offer diverse, convenient, high-quality and value-for-money home   老百姓一站式在家吃飯「多、快、好、省」的
meal products in a one-stop shop manner to meet consumers’         餐食產品，讓偏遠鄉村的老百姓也能吃到鍋
diverse needs under different dining scenarios, from urban         圈好吃方便還不貴的好食材。
centers to the most remote areas of China.

NON-IFRS MEASURES                                                      非國際財務報告準則計量
To supplement the Group’s consolidated financial information           為補充本集團根據國際財務報告準則編製及
prepared and presented in accordance with International                呈列的綜合財務資料，本集團亦採用經調整
Financial Reporting Standards, the Group has also adopted              淨利潤及經調整淨利潤率（各自為非國際財務
adjusted net profit and adjusted net profit margin (each a non-        報告準則計量）作為額外財務計量。經調整
IFRS measure) as additional financial measures. Adjusted               淨利潤指就上市開支做出調整後的年內淨利
net profit refers to net profit for the year adjusted for listing      潤。上市開支指與本集團於二零二三年完成
expenses. Listing expenses refer to the expenses relating to the       全球發售有關的費用。經調整淨利潤率乃按
global offering completed by the Group in 2023. Adjusted net           年內經調整淨利潤除以有關年度的總收入計
profit margin is calculated by dividing the adjusted net profit for    算。
the year by the total revenue for the relevant year.


The Group also uses core operating profit and core operating           本集團亦採用核心經營利潤及核心經營利潤
margin (each a non-IFRS measure) as additional financial               率（各自為非國際財務報告準則計量）作為額
measures to further exclude items affecting comparability.             外財務計量，以進一步排除影響可比性的項
We consider both quantitative and qualitative factors when             目。在評估是否對可能重大或可能影響對我
assessing whether to adjust for the impact of items that may           們持續財務和業務表現或趨勢的理解的項目
be material or may affect the understanding of our ongoing             的影響進行調整時，我們會考慮定量和定性
financial and business performance or trends. Items that are           因素。因受外界因素影響以及與我們核心營
affected by external factors and factors that are different            運不同或無關的因素影響，使得其被視為顯
from or unrelated to our core operations, such that they are           著影響本期或可比期間業績，通常被認為是
considered to significantly affect the results for the current         「影響可比性的項目」。影響可比性的項目包
or comparable periods, are generally considered to be “items           括：(i)上市開支；及(ii)非上市可轉換可贖回
affecting comparability”. Items affecting comparability include:       優先股的公允價值變動損益。核心經營利潤
(i) listing expenses; and (ii) gains or losses on fair value changes   率乃按報告期間內核心經營利潤除以報告期
on unlisted convertible redeemable preferred shares. Core              間的總收入計算。我們認為，呈列核心經營
operating margin is calculated by dividing core operating profit       利潤及核心經營利潤率為進一步加強我們過
for the Reporting Period by total revenue for the Reporting Period.    往經營業績與其相關經營業績趨勢的可比性
We believe that the presentation of core operating profit and core     提供了額外資料。
operating profit margin provides additional information to further
enhance the comparability of our historical results of operations
with the trends in our underlying results of operations.

     information and to assess the Group’s financial performance by        及透過撇除本集團認為並非本集團業務表現

     Group considers to be non-indicators of the Group’s business          的財務表現。本集團經營所在行業的其他公
     performance. Other companies in the industries in which the           司的非國際財務報告準則計量可能與本集團
     Group operates may have non-IFRS measures that are different          不同。非國際財務報告準則計量作為分析工
     from those of the Group. The use of non-IFRS measures poses           具存在局限性， 閣下不應將有關計量視為獨
     limitations as an analysis tool, you should not regard such           立於或可替代本集團根據國際財務報告準則
     measures as being independent of, or a substitute for, the            所呈報經營業績或財務狀況的分析。本集團
     analysis of the Group’s results of operations or financial position   呈列該非國際財務報告準則項目不應被視為
     as presented in accordance with IFRSs. The Group’s presentation       本集團未來業績將不受不尋常或非經常性項
     of such non-IFRS items should not be regarded as an inference         目影響的推斷。
     that the Group’s future results will not be affected by unusual or

FINANCIAL RISK                                                         財務風險
The Group is not subject to significant credit risk and liquidity      本集團並無面臨重大信貸風險及流動性風
risk. The Group is exposed to interest rate risk in relation to cash   險。本集團面臨與現金及銀行結餘、銀行借
and bank balances, bank borrowings. The Group considers that           款有關的利率風險。本集團認為，整體利率
the overall interest rate risk to be insignificant. The Group has      風險屬不重大。本集團在銀行存有外幣現
cash at bank in foreign currencies, which exposes the Group to         金，使本集團面臨外匯風險。本集團並無使
foreign exchange risk. The Group does not use any derivative           用任何衍生合約以對沖外匯風險。本集團透
contracts to hedge against foreign exchange risk. The Group            過密切監控外幣匯率的變動來管理其外匯風
manages its foreign exchange risk by closely monitoring the            險，並將採取謹慎措施將貨幣折算風險降至
movement of foreign exchange rates and will take prudent               最低。
measures to minimize the currency translation risk.

SIGNIFICANT INVESTMENTS, MATERIAL                                     重大投資、重大收購及出售
ACQUISITIONS AND DISPOSALS
The Company completed the acquisition of 41% equity interests         本公司於二零二四年十二月二十四日完成收
in Beihai Daixiaji Food Co., Ltd. (“Daixiaji”) on 24 December         購北海逮蝦記食品有限公司（「逮蝦記」）41%
2024 at a total purchase price of RMB246 million, which was           股權，收購價款合計為人民幣246 百萬元，
funded by the Company’s internal resources and in part by the         以本公司內部資源以及部分採用全球發售所
proceeds from the Global Offering. Following the completion of        得款項撥付。本次收購完成後，本集團合計
the acquisition, the Group held a total of 51% equity interest in     持有逮蝦記51%的股權，逮蝦記作為本公司
Daixiaji. As an associate of the Company, the portion of Daixiaji’s   的聯營公司，其淨利潤中歸屬於本集團的部
net profit attributable to the Group was recognized in Group’s        分在本集團的綜合損益表中確認。
consolidated statement of profit or loss.


Daixiaji is principally engaged in the production, research and       逮蝦記主要從事蝦滑、牛肉滑、豬肉滑、雞
development and sales of shrimp paste, beef paste, pork paste,        肉滑、墨魚滑、魚滑等滑類的生產、研發
chicken paste, cuttlefish paste, fish paste and other paste food,     以及銷售業務，以蝦滑為主要品類，以其他
with shrimp paste as the main category and other paste products       滑類豐富產品矩陣，和本集團有較好的協同
to enrich the product matrix, which has a good synergistic effect     效應。本集團一直致力於食品安全及品質
with the Group. The Group has always been committed to food           保證，對供應商有高要求，本次收購得以
safety and quality assurance, with high requirements for its          加大本集團在單品單廠戰略下，對於大單品
suppliers. The acquisition enabled the Group to enhance its           的上游供應鏈方面的控制力，並加強本集團
control over the upstream supply chain of key products under its      獲得優質且符合食品安全標準產品供應的穩
one-product-one-factory strategy and to strengthen its stability      定性，從而加深本集團的上游供應鏈市場佈
of obtaining the supply of high-quality products that meet food       局，深化協同效應和資源整合，進一步提升
safety standards, so as to intensify the Group’s upstream supply      本集團整體盈利能力。
chain market layout, deepen the synergy and resource integration
and further improve the Group’s overall profitability.


Daixiaji is a substantial associate of the Group and was recorded     逮蝦記為本集團的重要聯營公司，以權益法
by equity method. As at 31 December 2024, the carrying amount         入賬。於二零二四年十二月三十一日，本集
of investment in Daixiaji of the Group was RMB299.5 million,          團於逮蝦記的投資賬面值為人民幣299.5 百
representing approximately 6.3% of the Group’s total assets as at     萬元，佔本集團於二零二四年十二月三十一
31 December 2024.                                                     日資產總值的約6.3%。

     MAJOR RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                           主要風險及不確定因素
     The Directors are aware that the Group’s financial position,            董事知悉本集團的財務狀況，經營業績、及
     results of operations, and business prospects may be affected by        業務前景可能受到本集團業務直接或間接相
     a number of risks and uncertainties relating directly or indirectly     關的多項風險及不確定因素影響。我們面臨

           Reasons and benefits for transaction                           進行交易的理由及裨益

           Huading Cold Chain is a stable and reliable supplier of the    華鼎冷鏈為本集團穩定可靠的供應
           Group, possessing specialized expertise and experience in      商，具備專門針對消費零售業的專業
           the cold chain logistics for the consumer retail industry.     冷鏈物流的能力及經驗。華鼎冷鏈及
           The quality and efficiency of the cold chain warehousing       其聯繫人所提供冷鏈倉儲物流服務的
           and logistics services provided by Huading Cold Chain          質量和效率能滿足我們的物流需求，
           and its associates can meet our logistics requirements,        特別是華鼎冷鏈及其聯繫人能夠憑藉
           particularly through their centralized and integrated          其集中整合的冷鏈物流服務業務以相
           cold chain logistics services operations that allow for the    對較低成本高效、及時地提供遍佈全
           efficient and timely provision of nationwide integrated        國的一體化數字化冷鏈倉儲物流服
           digital cold chain warehousing and logistics services at a     務。與第三方一體化冷鏈倉儲物流服
           relatively low cost. Compared to the third-party providers     務供應商相比，華鼎冷鏈對我們的送
           of integrated cold chain warehousing and logistics             貨要求有更好的理解，對我們的全國

RESPONSIBILITIES OF AND DELEGATION BY THE                             董事會責任及授權
BOARD
The Board is responsible for the overall leadership of the Group,     董事會負責本集團的整體領導工作，監督本
oversees the Group’s strategic decisions, and monitors business       集團的戰略決策及監控業務和表現。
and performance.


The management, including executive Directors, is delegated with      管理層（包括執行董事）獲授權負責實施董事
responsibilities for implementing the strategy and direction as       會不時採納的策略及方針，以及負責本集團
adopted by the Board from time to time, and conducting the day        的日常營運，定期會面，以檢討本集團整體
– to – day operations of the Group. They meet regularly to review     業務表現、統籌整體資源及作出財務及營運
the performance of the businesses of the Group as a whole,            決策。董事會亦就其管理權力（包括管理層應
coordinate overall resources and make financial and operational       匯報的情況）作出明確指示，並將定期檢討授
decisions. The Board also gives clear directions as to their powers   權安排，以確保其仍然切合本集團的需要。
of management including circumstances where management
should report back, and will review the delegation arrangements
on a periodic basis to ensure that they remain appropriate to the
needs of the Group.

     BOARD COMMITTEES                                                    董事委員會
     To better serve the long-term interests of our stakeholders,        為使我們的持份者更能達致長期的利益，董
     the Board delegates certain matters requiring particular time,      事會就若干需要投入特定時間、注意力及專
     attention and expertise to its committees. The Board has            業知識的事宜指派給其委員會處理。董事會
     determined that these matters are better dealt with by the          認為該等事宜需要獨立監察及專家支持，故
     committees as they require independent oversight and specialist     由委員會處理更為適宜。因此，董事會已成
     input. As such, the Board has established three committees          立三個委員會，並已向委員會（包括審核委員
     and has delegated various responsibilities to the committees        會、薪酬委員會及提名委員會）授予多項職
     including Audit Committee, Remuneration Committee and               責。所有董事委員會均根據其各自的職權範
     Nomination Committee. All the Board committees perform              圍（可於本公司及聯交所網站查閱）履行各自
     their distinct roles in accordance with their respective terms of   的職責。董事委員會獲提供充足資源以履行
     reference which are available on the websites of the Company        其職責，並可應合理要求於適當情況下尋求
     and the Stock Exchange. The Board committees are provided           獨立專業意見，費用由本公司承擔。
     with sufficient resources to discharge their duties and, upon
     reasonable request, are able to seek independent professional
     advice in appropriate circumstances, at the Company’s expense.

     BOARD DIVERSITY                                                          董事會多元化
     The Company have adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board            為提升董事會的效率及維持高水準的企業管
     Diversity Policy”) which sets out the objective and approach             治，本公司已採用董事會多元化政策（「董
     to achieve and maintain diversity of the Board in order to               事會多元化政策」），其中載列實現和維持董
     enhance the effectiveness of the Board and to maintain the               事會多元化的目標和方法。根據董事會多元
     high standard of corporate governance. Pursuant to the Board             化政策，我們在甄選董事會候選人時，通過
     Diversity Policy, we seek to achieve diversity of our Board              考慮多項因素力求實現董事會多元化，包括
     through the consideration of a number of factors when selecting          但不限於專業經驗、技能、知識、性別、年
     candidates to the Board, including but not limited to professional       齡、文化、教育背景、種族及服務年期。本
     experience, skills, knowledge, gender, age, cultural and education       公司認同並深信董事會成員多元化的益處，
     background, ethnicity and length of service. The Company                 並視董事會層面日益多元化（包括性別多元
     recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board           化）為維持本公司競爭優勢及提升其吸納各類
     and sees increasing diversity at the Board level, including gender       不同人才及留聘及激勵員工的能力的關鍵因
     diversity, as an essential element in maintaining the Company’s          素。
     competitive advantage and enhancing its ability to attract,
     retain and motivate employees from the widest possible pool of
     available talent.

     The Board acknowledges that it is the responsibility of the          董事會確認，董事會有責任評估及釐定其在
     Board for evaluating and determining the nature and extent of        實現本集團戰略目標過程中願意承擔的風
     the risks it is willing to take in achieving the Group’s strategic   險性質及程度，並維持完善的風險管理及內
     objectives, and maintaining sound systems of risk management         部控制系統，以保障股東及本公司資產的利
     and internal controls in order to safeguard the interests of the     益，並持續且每年審閱該等系統的有效性。
     shareholders and the assets of the Company and also reviewing        董事會根據企業管治守則的規定建立風險管
     the effectiveness of such systems on an ongoing basis and            理及內部控制系統，該系統負責就本公司風
     annual basis. The Board has established a risk management and        險管理及內控控制系統的充足性及有效性進
     internal control system in accordance with the requirements of       行獨立審閱（涵蓋財務、營運及合規控制），
     the CG Code, which is responsible for independently reviewing the    並持續每年一次監督並審閱運作的有效性。
     adequacy and effectiveness of the risk management and internal       本集團的風險管理及內部監控系統旨在提供
     control system of the Company (covering financial, operational       合理（但並非絕對）的重大誤報或損失保證，
     and compliance controls), and continuously monitoring and            用來管理而非消除未能實現業務目標的風險。
     reviewing the effectiveness of its operation once a year. The
     Group’s risk management and internal control systems are
     designed to provide reasonable, though not absolute, assurance
     against material misstatement or loss and to manage rather than
     eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives.

any suspected improprieties in any matters related to the Group       疑似不當行為的任何事項，有關舉報會直接

OPINION                                                              意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of             我們已審計第116至219頁所載鍋圈食品（上
Guoquan Food (Shanghai) Co., Ltd. (the “Company”) and its            海）股份有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司
subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out on pages 116 to 219 which         （「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表，其中包括於
comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at      二零二四年十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況
31 December 2024, and the consolidated statement of profit or        表及截至該日止年度的綜合損益表、綜合全
loss, the consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the        面收益表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated     表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會計
statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and notes to the    政策資料。
consolidated financial statements, including material accounting
policy information.


In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a         我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據國際會
true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the     計準則理事會（「國際會計準則委員會」）頒佈
Group as at 31 December 2024, and of its consolidated financial      的國際財務報告會計準則真實而中肯地反映
performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then        了    貴集團於二零二四年十二月三十一日的

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不

made solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do         除此以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不會

person for the contents of this report.                                任何責任。

guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             香港審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken         財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               陳述可被視作重大。

      accordance with accordance with IFRS Accounting                         準則編製，而國際財務報告會計準則
      Standards, which comprise all standards and                             包括由國際會計準則理事會（「國際會

      Standards Board (the “IASB”), and International Accounting              釋常務委員會批准的準則及詮釋以及
      Standards and Standing Interpretations Committee                        香港公司條例的披露規定。
      interpretations approved by the International Accounting
      Standards Committee and the disclosure requirements of
      the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

      under the historical cost convention, except for financial              製，但按公允價值計入損益的金融資
      assets at fair value through profit or loss and equity                  產及指定按公允價值計入其他全面收
      investments designated at fair value through other                      益（「其他全面收益」）的股權投資除
      comprehensive income (“OCl”) which have been measured                   外，其乃按公允價值計量。綜合財務
      at fair value. The consolidated financial statements are                報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，除另
      presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded                有指明外，所有金額均約整至最接近
      to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.                的千位數。

      for the year ended 31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an                 止年度之財務報表。附屬公司指受本
      entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly,         公司直接或間接控制之實體（包括結
      controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when the                 構性實體）。倘本集團透過參與被投資
      Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its           方業務而享有或有權取得可變回報，
      involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect             且有能力行使對被投資方之權力影響

      existing rights that give the Group the current ability to              制權（即現時賦予本集團指導被投資
      direct the relevant activities of the investee).                        方相關活動之能力）。

2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                 2.4   重大會計政策
      Investments in associates                                          於聯營公司的投資

      An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long            聯營公司是指本集團長期持有其不少
      term interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity         於20%投票權且對其擁有重大影響力
      voting rights and over which it has significant influence.         的實體。重大影響力是指有權參與被
      Significant influence is the power to participate in the           投資公司的財務及經營政策的決策，
      financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,          但並非控制或共同控制該等政策。
      but is not control or joint control over those policies.


      The Group’s investments in associates are stated in                本集團於聯營公司的投資乃按權益會
      the consolidated statement of financial position at the            計法，在綜合財務狀況表內，按本集
      Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of             團應佔的資產淨值扣除任何減值虧損
      accounting, less any impairment losses. The Group’s share          呈列。本集團應佔聯營公司收購後業
      of the post-acquisition results and other comprehensive            績及其他全面收入份額分別計入綜合
      income of associates is included in the consolidated               損益表。此外，倘於聯營公司的權益
      statement of profit or loss. In addition, when there has           內直接確認一項變動，則本集團會於
      been a change recognised directly in the equity of the             綜合權益變動表內確認其於任何有關
      associate, the Group recognises its share of any changes,          變動的應佔份額（倘適用）。本集團與
      when applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes          聯營公司交易所產生的未變現盈虧，
      in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting from              均按本集團於聯營公司的投資為限進
      transactions between the Group and its associates are              行抵銷，除非未變現虧損證明已轉讓
      eliminated to the extent of the Group’s investments in             資產出現減值則除外。收購聯營公司
      the associates, except where unrealised losses provide             產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公司的
      evidence of an impairment of the assets transferred.               投資的一部分。
      Goodwill arising from the acquisition of associates is
      included as part of the Group’s investments in associates.

      2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   2.4   重大會計政策（續）
            (CONTINUED)
            Business combinations and goodwill                                   業務合併及商譽

            Business combinations are accounted for using the                    業務合併乃以收購法列賬。轉讓對價
            acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                 乃以收購日期的公允價值計量，該公
            measured at the acquisition date fair value which is                 允價值為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日
            the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets                期的公允價值、本集團向被收購方前
            transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the                 擁有人承擔的負債，及本集團發行以
            Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity            換取被收購方控制權的股本權益的總
            interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the         和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇是
            acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects            否以公允價值或被收購方可識別淨資
            whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the              產的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的非
            acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of the          控股權益。非控股權益的所有其他組
            acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components             成部分均按公允價值計量。收購相關
            of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.             成本於產生時列為開支。
            Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.


            The Group determines that it has acquired a business                 當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項
            when the acquired set of activities and assets includes an           資源投入及一項實質過程，而兩者對
            input and a substantive process that together significantly          創造產出的能力有重大貢獻時，本集
            contribute to the ability to create outputs.                         團釐定其已收購一項業務。


            When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                  當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合
            financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate             約條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相
            classification and designation in accordance with the                關條件，評估須承擔的金融資產及負
            contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent              債，以作出適合的分類及標示，其中
            conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the             包括將被收購方主合約中的嵌入式衍
            separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of              生工具進行分離。
            the acquiree.

      of the aggregate of the consideration transferred, the                 價、就非控股權益確認的金額及本集

      acquired, the difference is, after reassessment, recognised            確認為議價購買收益。
      in profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.

2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      2.4   重大會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)
      Fair value measurement                                                  公允價值計量

      The Group measures its certain financial assets and equity              本集團於報告期間末按公允價值計量
      investments at fair value at the end of the reporting period.           其若干金融資產及股權投資。公允價
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an               值為於計量日期在市場參與者間進行
      asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction         的有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉移
      between market participants at the measurement date.                    負債所支付的價格。公允價值計量乃
      The fair value measurement is based on the presumption                  假設出售資產或轉移負債的交易於資
      that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the                  產或負債的主要市場進行，或在未有
      liability takes place either in the principal market for the            主要市場的情況下，則假設於資產或
      asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal market,            負債的最有利市場進行。主要或最有
      in the most advantageous market for the asset or liability.             利市場必須為本集團可進入的市場。
      The principal or the most advantageous market must be                   資產或負債的公允價值乃基於市場參
      accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a                與者為資產或負債定價時所用的假設
      liability is measured using the assumptions that market                 計量（假設市場參與者以符合彼等最
      participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,             佳經濟利益的方式行事）。
      assuming that market participants act in their economic
      best interest.


      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes                 非金融資產的公允價值計量會計及一
      into account a market participant’s ability to generate                 名市場參與者透過以最大限度使用該
      economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and                 資產達致最佳用途，或透過將資產出
      best use or by selling it to another market participant that            售予將以最大限度使用該資產達致最
      would use the asset in its highest and best use.                        佳用途的另一名市場參與者而產生經
                                                                              濟利益的能力。


      The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate                本集團使用適用於不同情況且具備足
      in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                  夠可用數據以計量公允價值的估值方
      available to measure fair value, maximising the use of                  法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據
      relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                    及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
      unobservable inputs.

      assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows              計未來現金流量以反映當時市場對貨

      Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment                 如物業、廠房及設備項目各部分的可
      have different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated         使用年期各有不同，該項目的成本乃
      on a reasonable basis among the parts and each part is                  按合理基準在各部分之間進行分配，
      depreciated separately. Residual values, useful lives and               而每部分則各自計算折舊。剩餘價
      the depreciation method are reviewed, and adjusted if                   值、可使用年期及折舊方法至少會於
      appropriate, at least at each financial year end.                       各財政年結日進行審核及調整（如適
                                                                              用）。


      An item of property, plant and equipment including any                  物業、廠房及設備項目（包括已首次
      significant part initially recognised is derecognised upon              確認的任何重大部分）於出售時或預
      disposal or when no future economic benefits are expected               期日後會因使用或出售而還會帶來經
      from its use or disposal. Any gain or loss on disposal or               濟利益時終止確認。於資產終止確認
      retirement recognised in the statement of profit or loss                年度在損益表確認的出售或報廢的任
      in the year the asset is derecognised is the difference                 何盈虧為有關資產的出售所得款項淨
      between the net sales proceeds and the carrying amount                  額與其賬面值間的差額。
      of the relevant asset.


      Construction in progress is stated at cost less any                     在建工程按成本減任何減值虧損入賬
      impairment losses, and is not depreciated. It is reclassified           及且並無折舊。竣工及可作擬定用途
      to the appropriate category of property, plant and                      後，在建工程將在物業、廠房及設備
      equipment when completed and ready for use.                             項目內適當重新分類。

      economic life and assessed for impairment whenever there                示無形資產可能出現減值時評估減
      is an indication that the intangible asset may be impaired.             值。有限使用年期之無形資產之攤銷
      The amortisation period and the amortisation method for                 年限及攤銷方法最少於各財政年度末

      least at each financial year end.

      Impairment of financial assets                                      金融資產減值

      The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit               本集團確認對並非按公允價值計入損
      losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at                益的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損的撥
      fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the            備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約到
      difference between the contractual cash flows due in                期的合約現金流量與本集團預期收取
      accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                 的所有現金流量之間的差額而釐定，
      that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an                 並以原實際利率的近似值貼現。預期
      approximation of the original effective interest rate. The          現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的現金
      expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale           流量或組成合約條款的其他信貸提升

      integral to the contractual terms.

      2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                    2.4   重大會計政策（續）
            (CONTINUED)
            Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                            金融資產減值（續）

            General approach                                                      一般法

            ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for           預期信貸虧損分兩個階段進行確認。
            which there has not been a significant increase in credit             就自初步確認起未有顯著增加的信貸
            risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit          風險而言，預期信貸虧損提供予由未
            losses that result from default events that are possible              來12個月內可能發生違約事件而導致
            within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those                 的信貸虧損（12個月預期信貸虧損）。
            credit exposures for which there has been a significant               就自初步確認起經已顯著增加的信貸
            increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss             風險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘
            allowance is required for credit losses expected over the             下風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計
            remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of         提虧損準備（年限內預期信貸虧損）。
            the default (a lifetime ECL).


            At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                    在各報告日期，本集團評估自初步確
            the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased               認起金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著
            significantly since initial recognition. When making the              增加。進行評估時，本集團比較金融
            assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                  工具在報告日期發生違約的風險及金
            occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting             融工具在初始確認日期發生違約的風
            date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial            險，同時考慮無需沒有過多的成本和
            instrument as at the date of initial recognition and                  努力即可獲得的合理的和可支持的信
            considers reasonable and supportable information that is              息，包括歷史和前瞻性信息。本集團
            available without undue cost or effort, including historical          認為，當合同付款逾期超過30天時，
            and forward-looking information. The Group considers that             信貸風險會顯著增加。
            there has been a significant increase in credit risk when
            contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.


            The Group considers a financial asset in default when                 當合約付款逾期90天時，本集團將考
            contractual payments are 90 days past due. However, in                慮違約的金融資產。然而，在某些情
            certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial                況下，當內部或外部信息表明本集團
            asset to be in default when internal or external information          不太可能在考慮到本集團所持有的任
            indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the                   何信貸增強之前全額收到未付合約款
            outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into            項時，本集團也可將一項金融資產視
            account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                    為違約。


            A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable          金融資產在沒有合理預期收回合約現
            expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.                 金流的情況下被沖銷。

      2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                  2.4   重大會計政策（續）
            (CONTINUED)
            Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                          金融資產減值（續）

            Simplified approach                                                 簡化方法

            For trade receivables that do not contain a significant             對於不包含重要融資組成部分的貿易
            financing component or when the Group applies the                   應收款項，或當本集團採取實務中不
            practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a                調整重要融資組成部分的影響時，本
            significant financing component, the Group applies                  集團在計算預期信貸虧損時採用簡易
            the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the              法。根據簡易法，本集團不跟蹤信貸
            simplified approach, the Group does not track changes               風險的變化，而是在每個報告日期確
            in credit risk, but instead recognises a loss allowance             認基於全期預期信貸虧損計算虧損準
            based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group            備。本集團根據其歷史信貸虧損經驗
            has established a provision matrix that is based on its             建立了一個撥備矩陣，並根據債務人
            historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-            具體的前瞻性因素和經濟環境加以調
            looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic            整。
            environment.


            Trade and other payables                                            貿易及其他應付款項

            Trade and other payables are initially recognised at fair           貿易及其他應付款項初步按公允價值
            value. Subsequent to initial recognition, trade and other           確認。初次確認後，貿易及其他應付
            payables are stated at amortised cost unless the effect of          款項按攤銷成本入賬，但如貼現影響
            discounting would be immaterial, in which case they are             並不重大，則按票據金額入賬。
            stated at invoice amounts.


            Borrowings                                                          借款

            Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of           借款初始以公允價值扣減所產生的交
            transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently             易成本確認。借款隨後按攤銷成本列
            carried at amortised cost; any difference between the               賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖回
            proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption              價值之間的任何差額採用實際利息法
            value is recognised in the consolidated statements of               於借款期間在綜合損益表內確認。
            profit or loss over the period of the borrowings using the
            effective interest method.


            Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the         除非本集團擁有無條件權利將負債結
            Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of             算遞延報告期末後至少12個月，否則
            the liability for at least 12 months after the end of the           借款被分類為流動負債。
            reporting period.

2.4   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     2.4   重大會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)
      Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債

      A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation              當負債之責任解除或註銷或到期時，
      under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.            金融負債將終止確認。


      When an existing financial liability is replaced by another            若現有金融負債由另一項來自相同貸
      from the same lender on substantially different terms,                 方按完全不同之條款提供負債取代，
      or the terms of an existing liability are substantially                或現有負債之條款作出重大修訂，則
      modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as               上述取代或修訂視為終止確認原有負
      a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of         債及確認新負債，而相關賬面值之差
      a new liability, and the difference between the respective             額在損益表確認。
      carrying amounts is recognised in the statement of profit
      or loss.


      Inventories                                                            存貨

      Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                    存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較
      realisable value. Cost is determined on the moving                     低者列賬。成本以移動加權平均法計
      weighted average method and, in the case of work in                    算，而就在製品及製成品而言，成本
      progress and finished goods, comprises direct materials,               包括直接材料、直接勞工及適當比例
      direct labour and an appropriate proportion of overheads.              的間接成本。可變現淨值按估計售價
      Net realisable value is based on estimated selling prices              減完成及出售將產生的任何估計成本
      less any estimated costs to be incurred to completion and              計算。
      disposal.


      Cash and cash equivalents                                              現金及現金等價物

      For the purpose of the consolidated statements of cash                 就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金
      flows, cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on                      等價物包括手頭現金及活期存款以及
      hand and demand deposits, and short term highly liquid                 短期高度流動性之投資，該等投資可
      investments that are readily convertible into known                    隨時兌換為可知數額之現金，且毋須
      amounts of cash, are subject to an in significant risk of              承受價值變動之重大風險，並為較短
      changes in value, and have a short maturity of generally               期之投資項目（一般自購入日期起計
      within three months when acquired, less bank overdrafts                三個月內到期），並已減去須按要求償
      which are repayable on demand and form an integral part                還並組成本集團現金管理之主要部分
      of the Group’s cash management.                                        之銀行透支。


      For the purpose of the consolidated statements of                      就綜合財務狀況表而言，現金及現金
      financial position, cash and cash equivalents comprise                 等價物包括用途不受限制的手頭及銀
      cash on hand and at banks, including term deposits, and                行現金（包括定期存款）以及性質類似
      assets similar in nature to cash, which are not restricted             現金的資產。
      as to use.

            Provisions                                                           撥備

            A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal           倘若因過往事件導致現有債務（法定
            or constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and          或推定），且日後很可能需要有資源流
            it is probable that a future outflow of resources will be            出以償還債務，則確認撥備，但必須
            required to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable          能可靠估計有關債務金額。
            estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.


            When the effect of discounting is material, the amount               倘若貼現的影響重大，則確認的撥備
            recognised for a provision is the present value at the end           金額為預期需用作償還債務的未來支
            of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected          出於報告期末的現值。因時間流逝而
            to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the         產生的貼現現值增加部分，列作財務
            discounted present value amount arising from the passage             成本計入損益表內。
            of time is included in finance costs in the statement of
            profit or loss.


            Income tax                                                           所得稅

            Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income                所得稅包括當期及遞延稅項。有關在
            tax relating to items recognised outside profit or loss              損益以外確認項目之所得稅在損益以
            is recognised outside profit or loss, either in other                外確認，計入其他全面收益或直接計
            comprehensive income or directly in equity.                          入權益內。


            Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at the               當期稅項資產或負債按預期可獲稅務
            amount expected to be recovered from or paid to the                  當局退回或付予稅務當局之金額，根
            taxation authorities, based on tax rates (and tax laws)              據報告期末已實施或已實際實施之稅
            that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the               率（及稅法）計算，而計算時亦考慮到
            end of the reporting period, taking into consideration               本集團經營所在國家現行之詮釋及慣
            interpretations and practices prevailing in the countries in         例。
            which the Group operates.


            Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all         遞延稅項為就財務申報而言，於報告
            temporary differences at the end of the reporting period             期末就資產及負債之稅基及彼等賬面
            between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their            值之所有暫時性差異，利用負債法作
            carrying amounts for financial reporting purposes.                   出撥備。

      The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                遞延稅項資產之賬面值乃於報告期末
      the end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent                進行審閱，並予以相應扣減，直至不
      that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profit             可能有足夠應課稅利潤以動用全部或
      will be available to allow all or part of the deferred tax               部分遞延稅項資產為止。未確認之遞
      asset to be utilised. Unrecognised deferred tax assets                   延稅項資產於報告期末重新評估，並

      recognised to the extent that it has become probable that                部或部分遞延稅項資產之情況下予以
      sufficient taxable profit will be available to allow all or part         確認。
      of the deferred tax asset to be recovered.


      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the                  遞延稅項資產及負債乃根據於報告期
      tax rates that are expected to apply to the period when                  末已實施或已實際實施之稅率（及稅
      the asset is realised or the liability is settled, based                 法），按變現資產或清償負債之期間預
      on tax rates (and tax laws) that have been enacted or                    期適用之稅率予以計量。
      substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.


      Deferred tax assets and deferred tax liabilities are offset              當且僅當：本集團存在可依法強制執
      if and only if the Group has a legally enforceable right to              行的權利，可將即期稅項資產與負債
      set off current tax assets and current tax liabilities and               相抵銷；與所得稅相關之遞延稅項資
      the deferred tax assets and deferred tax liabilities relate              產與負債涉及同一應課稅實體及同一
      to income taxes levied by the same taxation authority on                 稅務機關，或涉及本集團內不同應課
      either the same taxable entity or different taxable entities             稅實體（同一稅務機關），該等實體計
      which intend either to settle current tax liabilities and                劃將即期稅項資產與負債按淨額基準
      assets on a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle               相抵銷，或在未來預計變現重大遞延
      the liabilities simultaneously, in each future period in                 稅項資產或清償遞延稅項負債的每一
      which significant amounts of deferred tax liabilities or                 會計期間，同時變現資產或清償負債
      assets are expected to be settled or recovered.                          時，遞延稅項資產及遞延稅項負債方
                                                                               可互相抵銷。

      costs, for which it is intended to compensate are expensed.

            Revenue recognition                                                 收入確認

            Revenue from contracts with customers                               客戶合約收入

            Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised                 客戶合約收入乃於商品或服務的控制
            when control of goods or services is transferred to the             權轉讓予客戶時確認，該金額能反映

            to which the Group expects to be entitled in exchange for           權獲得的對價。
            those goods or services.


            When the consideration in a contract includes a variable            當合約中的對價包含可變金額時，對
            amount, the amount of consideration is estimated to which           價金額於本集團向客戶轉讓貨品或服

            constrained until it is highly probable that a significant          決時，確認的累計收入金額極有可能
            revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue                不會發生重大收入回撥。
            recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty
            with the variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

            year, revenue recognised under the contract includes the            付款至轉讓承諾貨品或者服務的期限

            the effective interest method. For a contract where the             格採用國際財務報告準則第15號中實

32.   RESERVES                                                        32.   儲備
      The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                 本集團的儲備及其變動於財務報表的
      therein are presented in the consolidated statements of               綜合權益變動表內呈列。
      changes in equity in financial statements.


      (i)    Statutory reserve                                              (i)    法定儲備

             In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC,                        根據中國公司法，在中國註冊
             companies registered in the PRC are required to                       的公司須將法定除稅後利潤的
             allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits to the                10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲
             statutory reserve until the cumulative total of the                   備的累計總額達到公司註冊資
             reserve reaches 50% of the companies registered                       本的50%。待中國有關當局批
             capital. Subject to approval from the relevant PRC                    准後，法定儲備可用於抵銷任
             authorities, the statutory reserve may be used                        何累計虧損或增加公司的註冊
             to offset any accumulated losses or increase the                      資本。法定儲備不可用於向中
             registered capital of the companies. The statutory                    國附屬公司權益持有人分派股
             reserve is not available for dividend distribution to                 息。
             equity holders of the PRC subsidiaries.


      (ii)   Capital reserve                                                (ii)   資本儲備

             The capital reserve of the Group represents                           本集團的資本儲備指於過往年
             the excess of the consideration received for                          度就認購本公司註冊資本所收
             subscription of the registered capital of the                         取的對價超過控股股東楊明超
             Company and costs borne by the controlling                            先生、孟先進先生及李欣華先
             shareholders, Mr. Yang Mingchao, Mr. Meng Xianjin                     生所承擔的成本的部分。
             and Mr. Li Xinhua in prior years.

      39.   FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF                         39.   金融工具的公允價值及公允價
            FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                                值等級
            Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and             管理層已評定現金及銀行結餘、受限
            bank balances, restricted cash, trade receivables, financial         制現金、貿易應收款項、計入預付款
            assets included in prepayments, other receivables and                項、其他應收款項及其他資產的金融
            other assets, interest-bearing bank and other borrowings,            資產、計息銀行及其他借款、貿易應
            trade payables, financial liabilities included in other              付款項、計入其他應付款項及應計費
            payables and accruals approximate to their carrying                  用的金融負債的公允價值與其賬面值
            amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of these            相若，主要由於該等工具的短期到期
            instruments.                                                         情況所致。


            The Group’s corporate finance team is responsible for                本集團的企業財務團隊負責釐定金融
            determining the policies and procedures for the fair value           工具公允價值管理的政策及程序。企
            management of financial instruments. The corporate                   業財務團隊直接向財務總監及董事會
            finance team reports directly to the chief financial officer         匯報。於各報告日期，企業財務團隊
            and the board of directors. At each reporting date, the              分析金融工具價值的變動，並釐定估
            corporate finance team analyses the movements in the                 值所應用的主要輸入數據。估值由財
            values of financial instruments and determines the major             務總監審核及批准。估值過程及結果
            inputs applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed           乃與董事會討論以作出年度財務報告。
            and approved by the chief financial officer. The valuation
            process and results are discussed with the board of
            directors for annual financial reporting.


            The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are          金融資產及負債的公允價值按自願訂
            included at the amount at which the instrument could be              約方（強迫或清盤出售除外）當前交易
            exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties,          中該工具的可交換金額入賬。在估計
            other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following            其公允價值時已採用下列方法及假設。
            methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair
            values.


            The fair value of long-term bank deposits has been                   長期銀行存款的公允價值乃採用具有
            calculated by discounting the expected future cash flows             類似條款、信貸風險及剩餘到期日的
            using rates currently available for instruments with similar         工具當前適用的現行利率貼現預期未
            terms, credit risk and remaining maturities. The carrying            來現金流量計算。長期銀行存款的賬
            amounts of long-term bank deposits approximate to their              面值與其公允價值相若。
            fair values.

39.   FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF                       39.   金融工具的公允價值及公允價
      FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (CONTINUED)                                  值等級（續）
      The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent         本集團投資於非上市投資，即中國內
      wealth management products issued by banks in Mainland             地銀行發行的理財產品。本集團根據
      China. The Group has estimated the fair value of wealth            具有類似條款及風險的工具的市場利
      management products by using a discounted cash flow                率，使用貼現現金流量估值模型估計
      valuation model based on the market interest rates of              理財產品的公允價值。
      instruments with similar terms and risks.


      The fair values of listed equity investments are based on          上市股權投資的公允價值基於市場報
      quoted market prices. For the fair value of the unlisted           價。就按公允價值計入其他全面收益
      equity investments at fair value through OCI, management           的非上市股權投資的公允價值而言，
      has estimated the potential effect of using reasonably             管理層已估計使用合理可行的替代方
      possible alternatives as inputs to the valuation model.            法（作為估值模式的輸入數據）的潛在
                                                                         影響。

      40.   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                             40.   財務風險管理目標及政策
            AND POLICIES
            The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise bank              本集團的主要金融工具包括銀行及其
            and other interest-bearing borrowings, long-term bank                  他計息借款、長期銀行存款以及現金
            deposits and cash and bank balances. The main purpose                  及銀行結餘。該等金融工具的主要目
            of these financial instruments is to raise finance for the             的是為本集團的營運籌集資金。本集
            Group’s operations. The Group has various other financial              團有多項其他金融資產及負債，如直
            assets and liabilities such as trade receivables and trade             接自其營運產生的貿易應收款項及貿
            payables, which arise directly from its operations.                    易應付款項。


            The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                      本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外
            instruments are foreign currency risk, interest rate risk,             幣風險、利率風險、信貸風險及流動
            credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews         資金風險。董事會審閱及同意管理該
            and agrees policies for managing each of these risks and               等各項風險的政策，其概要如下。
            they are summarised below.


            Foreign currency risk                                                  外幣風險

            The Group’s major businesses are carried out in Mainland               本集團主要業務於中國內地開展，大
            China and most of the transactions are conducted in RMB.               部分交易以人民幣進行。本集團大
            Most of the Group’s assets and liabilities are denominated             部分資產及負債以人民幣計值。本集
            in RMB. The Group does not have material foreign currency              團並無重大外幣風險。於二零二四年
            risk. As at 31 December 2024, the Group’s assets and                   十二月三十一日，本集團以港元計值
            liabilities denominated in HK$ were mainly held by the                 得資產及負債主要由本公司及若干附
            Company and certain subsidiaries incorporated outside                  屬公司（於中國內地以外地區註冊成
            Mainland China which had currencies other than RMB                     立，並以人民幣以外的其他貨幣作為
            as their functional currencies. The Company and those                  功能貨幣）持有。本公司及附屬公司
            subsidiaries incorporated outside Mainland China also                  （於中國內地以外地區註冊成立）亦持
            held bank balances denominated in RMB, from which                      有以人民幣計值的銀行結餘，從而產
            foreign currency exposures arise.                                      生外幣風險。

            who wish to trade on credit terms are subject to credit                      信貸期進行交易的客戶均須通過信貸
            verification procedures. In addition, receivable balances                    核實程序。此外，本集團持續監控應
            are monitored on an ongoing basis and the Group’s                            收款項結餘，而本集團所面對的壞賬
            exposure to bad debts is not significant.                                    風險並不重大。

40.   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                          40.   財務風險管理目標及政策（續）
      AND POLICIES (CONTINUED)
      Capital management                                                  資本管理

      The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management            本集團資本管理的主要目標是維護本
      are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going         集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健資本
      concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to          充足率以支持業務發展及實現權益持
      support its business and maximise equity holders’ value.            有人價值最大化。


      The Group manages its capital structure and makes                   本集團會根據經濟狀況變化和相關資
      adjustments to it in light of changes in economic                   產的風險特徵管理或調整資本結構。
      conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying           為維持或調整資本結構，本集團會調
      assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the            整派發予權益持有人的股息、返還股
      Group may adjust the dividend payment to equity holders,            本予權益持有人或發行新股。本集團
      return capital to equity holders or issue new shares. The           並無受到任何外部資本需求的約束。
      Group is not subject to any externally imposed capital              於截至二零二四年十二月三十一日及
      requirements. No changes were made in the objectives,               二零二三年十二月三十一日止年度，
      policies or processes for managing capital during the years         本集團並無對資本管理的目標、政策
      ended 31 December 2024 and 31 December 2023.                        或程序作出變動。


      The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which             本集團使用資產負債比率監控資本，
      is debt divided by the adjusted capital plus debt. Debt             資產負債比率為負債除以經調整資
      includes interest-bearing borrowings and lease liabilities.         本加上負債。負債包括計息借款和租
      Capital represents equity attributable to owners of the             賃負債。資本指母公司擁有人應佔權
      parent. The gearing ratios as at the end of the reporting           益。於報告期末的資產負債比率如
      period were as follows:                                             下：
